How to read this. Items are grouped by what is being asked of you. Decide means a judgement is needed. Confirm means something was changed automatically and should be checked. Informational means no action is expected. Whitespace is shown with visible marks: · a space, ⇥ a tab, ␣ a non-breaking space, ¶ a line break.

Confirm Text changed: repeated space in display name: codedContent.displayName 66

Every run of two or more spaces in a code display name is collapsed, wherever it sits, per ADR-0008 D4. Display names are short labels, so no run of repeated spaces in one is meaningful. These were changed.
